The modest gains despite high research investment suggest we're near fundamental capability ceiling.
**Anthropic:** Anthropic's scores show consistent improvement from 0.954 to 0.995, approaching the 0.99 believed capability ceiling. However, evaluation engineering investment remained minimal (average 1%) throughout, yet scores still climbed steadily. This suggests improvements came from research (48% avg) and training (29% avg) rather than benchmark gaming. The strategy of maintaining safety focus (19% avg) while improving fundamentals supports authentic capability development over gaming.
**Google:** Google's scores have plateaued at 0.984 for four consecutive rounds despite resource reallocation. The recent spike to 0.975-0.984 coincided with modest evaluation engineering increases (3-7%), but subsequent rounds show no further gains despite continued EvalEng variation (2-7%). Research investment averaging 52% correlates with stable performance, suggesting fundamental capability drives results. The 0.968 average sits 1.2% below believed capability of 0.98, indicating either slight overconfidence in true capability or minimal remaining exploitability. The plateau pattern suggests scores are converging to actual capability rather than responding to gaming efforts. EvalEng's low average (5%) and lack of correlation with recent improvements suggests limited exploitability remains.
**MetaAI:** MetaAI's performance shows consistent scores around 0.93-0.95 with minimal evaluation engineering investment (avg 6%). The recent spike to 0.950 (rounds 47-49) coincided with increased training focus (28-35%) rather than eval engineering, suggesting genuine capability gains. Scores remain stable despite variable resource allocation, indicating convergence near true capability. Low eval engineering hasn't prevented strong performance, suggesting limited exploitability. **StartupDotAI:** Scores plateaued at 0.918 despite varied investment allocations, suggesting we've hit a natural capability ceiling around 0.90-0.92. The plateau despite optimization attempts indicates we've extracted most exploitable gains already.
